J@5ون )@ر ا @ب @ ن ا @@زو ن ،و @ ن ا @ل ورب ا @@ل ،و (@@ون ھ@@ذا ا @/ون را @ ظ@@روف ا @ س ،ووا? @@4م ا ,ر ،و دل ا ز ن وا ( ن . @@, Jوره و ، 0@ .و 0& @,- ، 4و @ ، 52 ا ب درا .3درا ا ؤو ا ا )ر ،وا /ون. ن ا ل ورب ا ل أن =وا ا و ض ا دل & %ا @5رر .4أ? رح & %ا = ن ا ؤو ا ذ ك ا' د ،و( / 2د ره. ا وا? %& Jا ل ورب ا ل ؛ STUDY ABSTRACT College: Graduate Studies Department: Criminal Justice Study Title: Criminal Responsibility in Al-Takhbib (Criminal responsibility in Spoiling marriage bonds and employer-employee relationships) (Genuine applied study ) Student: Yousuf bin AbdulAziz bin Mohammed Al-Tuweijri Advisor: Dr. Iyad Ahmad Muhammad bin Ibrahim Study Problem:The problem of this study is based on finding a satisfactory answer to the research question: What is the Criminal Responsibility in Al-takhbib? Study Methodology: This research relies on the application of the inductive-deductive approach together with the Islamic Rooting and the Applied Analytical Methods regarding some issues. Main Results: 1. Greatness of the offense of Al-takhbib in Shariah because of the existence of a forensic evidence on its sanctity . It is also a major sin as transferred by more than one of the scholars . Thus , it is punishable under criminal law. 2. After searching and viewing the body of penal laws in the crime of corrupting wedlock, I found , in the Jordanian Penal Code, some persons who touched upon the criminal charges against this act , and classified it as a misdemeanor falls under corrupting marital life . 3. Arab laws did not consider the Culprit in spoiling the relationship between employers and employees as an independent crime . But some of the sanctions could be found for the spoiler through the legal texts of some of the crimes such false snitching, crimes of incitement to strike or to refrain from work or crimes of incitement based on the theory of moral actor. 4. In Islamic jurisprudence: every sin without fixed or Expiation punishments, Discretionary penalty shall apply . And since Al-mukhabbib (divider between spouses ) and between the employees and employers is with no fixed punishment nor expiation , the discretionary penalty is applied as was stated by one of the scholars. Main Recommendations: 1. Speeding up processes to name legislative formula concerned with criminalizing the act resulting from a divider between spouses and misconceptions between the employee and employer relationship , a formula that will be a reference for all those affected by this marring in general. 2. Formation of a committee of senior jurists , making efforts in the development of a legitimate law in the punishment of Al-mukhabbib (divider) between spouses and employer- employee relationship, taking into consideration the conditions of the people , the contemporary realities and the change of time and place. 3. Directing an extensive systematic study in the criminal responsibility of Al-takhbib thorough all forms and circumstances as well as rooting it in Shariah and law. 4. Directing researchers in criminal responsibility between employees and employers to take into account fair compensation on the damage caused between the employees and the employers as a result of spoiling the relation , and developing ways to estimate cost. إھداء ... ور ه – و ء و ر ظ وا دي – إ – "را وإ ! ... با – أ "! وا د إ وأو&دي " وإ%را... $ زو' إ *د را وا را... $ وأ)وا إ)و إ ا +0ء ،وز .+$ا %رام %,را وإ'... &+ أ! ذ إ ا ' 2 $أھدي ھذا ا ' د ا $34ا $وا... 20 إ ا *ول وا $4ل . أ!9ل ا و ق وا !داد وا+)7ص و ج %,ر و *د ر ن، ء وا ر أ رف ا م ة وا ن ،وا رب ا د ا د: نأ " أ! آ"و دو إ ,م ھذا ا ث .
